<style>
:root {
  --circle-size: 48px; /* यहाँबाट circle को size बदल्न सकिन्छ */
}

.marquee-container {
  overflow: hidden;
  white-space: nowrap;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  border: 2px solid #0b5cff;
  background: #372dec28;
  padding: 10px 0;
  width: 100%;
}

.marquee-content {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  padding-left: 100%;
  font-family: "Noto Sans Devanagari", sans-serif;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 2rem;
  
  background: linear-gradient(
    90deg,
    red, orange, yellow, green, blue, indigo, violet
  );
  background-size: 700% 100%;
  background-clip: text;
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  color: transparent;

  animation: marqueeAnim 15s linear infinite, rainbowShift 10s linear infinite;
  user-select: none;
}

.circle-img {
  display: inline-block;
  width: var(--circle-size);
  height: var(--circle-size);
  margin: 0 12px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow:
    0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.3),
    inset 0 2px 4px rgba(255,255,255,0.6);
  background: linear-gradient(145deg, #ffffff, #d1d9e6);
  filter: drop-shadow(0 2px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.2));
  flex-shrink: 0;
  transform-style: preserve-3d;
  transition: transform 0.3s ease;
}

.circle-img img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: block;
}

.circle-img:hover {
  transform: rotateY(20deg) rotateX(10deg) scale(1.1);
  box-shadow:
    0 6px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.4),
    inset 0 3px 6px rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
}

@keyframes marqueeAnim {
  0% {
    transform: translateX(0);
  }
  100% {
    transform: translateX(-100%);
  }
}

@keyframes rainbowShift {
  0% {
    background-position: 0% 50%;
  }
  100% {
    background-position: 700% 50%;
  }
}
</style>













<style>
:root{
  --blue:#0b5cff;
  --red:#ff004f;
  --bg:#f4f7ff;
  --radius:14px;
}
*{box-sizing:border-box;font-family:system-ui}
body{background:var(--bg)}
.hidden{display:none}
button{border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 14px;cursor:pointer}
.btn{background:var(--blue);color:#fff}
.red{background:var(--red);color:#fff}
input,textarea,select{
  width:100%;padding:8px;margin:6px 0;
  border:1px solid #ccc;border-radius:8px
}
.layout{display:flex;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.box{
  background:#fff;padding:14px;border-radius:var(--radius);
  flex:1;min-width:300px
}
.toolbar button{background:#eee;margin-right:4px}
.post{border-bottom:1px dashed #ccc;padding:10px 0}
img.postimg{max-width:100%;margin-top:8px;border-radius:10px}
.a4{width:210mm;max-width:100%}
.status{font-size:12px;color:#666}
.share a{margin-right:10px;font-size:13px}
.badge{font-size:11px;background:#eee;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:6px}
</style>

<div class="layout">

<!-- ================= जन–आवाज ================= -->
<div class="box">
<h3>🔊 जन–आवाज</h3>

<div class="toolbar">
<button onclick="fmt('bold')">B</button>
<button onclick="fmt('italic')">I</button>
<button onclick="fmt('underline')">U</button>
<select onchange="fsize(this.value)">
<option value="">Size</option>
<option value="14px">Small</option>
<option value="18px">Medium</option>
<option value="24px">Large</option>
</select>
<input type="color" onchange="fcolor(this.value)">
</div>

<div id="vText" contenteditable
 style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:10px;border-radius:8px;min-height:90px"
 data-placeholder="यहाँ आफ्नो आवाज लेख्नुहोस्..."></div>

<input id="vName" placeholder="नाम *">
<input id="vPhone" placeholder="फोन *">
<input type="file" id="vFile" accept="image/*">
<select id="vImgSize">
<option value="real">Real Size</option>
<option value="a4">A4 Size</option>
</select>
<button class="btn" onclick="submitVoice()">Submit</button>
</div>

<!-- ================= प्रेस ================= -->
<div class="box">
<h3>📰 प्रेस</h3>
<button class="btn" onclick="togglePress()">नयाँ प्रेस</button>

<div id="pressEditor" class="hidden">
<div class="toolbar">
<button onclick="fmt('bold')">B</button>
<button onclick="fmt('italic')">I</button>
<button onclick="fmt('underline')">U</button>
<select onchange="fsize(this.value)">
<option value="">Size</option>
<option value="16px">Normal</option>
<option value="22px">Large</option>
<option value="28px">Extra</option>
</select>
<input type="color" onchange="fcolor(this.value)">
</div>

<div id="pText" contenteditable
 style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:10px;border-radius:8px;min-height:100px"></div>

<input type="file" id="pFile" accept="image/*">
<select id="pImgSize">
<option value="real">Real Size</option>
<option value="a4">A4 Size</option>
</select>
<button class="btn" onclick="submitPress()">Save</button>
</div>
</div>

<!-- ================= Admin ================= -->
<div class="box">
<h3>🔐 Admin</h3>
<input id="aid" placeholder="Admin ID">
<input id="apass" type="password" placeholder="Password">
<button class="btn" onclick="login()">Login</button>

<div id="adminPanel" class="hidden">
<h4>⏳ Pending</h4>
<div id="pending"></div>
<h4>✅ Approved</h4>
<div id="approved"></div>
<button class="red" onclick="logout()">Logout</button>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="box" style="margin-top:14px">
<h3>📢 सार्वजनिक पोस्ट</h3>
<div id="public"></div>
</div>

<script>
/* ================= CORE STORAGE ================= */
const DB={
  voices:JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("voices")||"[]"),
  press:JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("press")||"[]")
};

/* ================= SECURITY HELPERS ================= */
function sanitize(html){
  const d=document.createElement("div");
  d.textContent=html;
  return d.innerHTML;
}

/* ================= TEXT FORMAT ================= */
function fmt(c){document.execCommand(c)}
function fsize(s){
  document.execCommand("fontSize",false,7);
  document.querySelectorAll('font[size="7"]').forEach(f=>f.style.fontSize=s);
}
function fcolor(c){document.execCommand("foreColor",false,c)}

/* ================= VALIDATION ================= */
function validPhone(p){return /^[0-9+]{7,15}$/.test(p)}

/* ================= SUBMIT VOICE ================= */
function submitVoice(){
  if(!vText.innerText.trim()||!vName.value||!validPhone(vPhone.value)){
    alert("सबै आवश्यक जानकारी सही भर्नुहोस्");
    return;
  }
  let img="";
  if(vFile.files[0]){
    if(vFile.files[0].size>2*1024*1024){alert("Image too large");return;}
    let u=URL.createObjectURL(vFile.files[0]);
    img=`<img class="postimg ${vImgSize.value==='a4'?'a4':''}" src="${u}">`;
  }
  DB.voices.push({
    html:sanitize(vText.innerHTML)+img,
    name:vName.value,
    phone:vPhone.value,
    approved:false,
    hidden:false,
    date:Date.now()
  });
  saveDB();
  alert("Submit भयो (Admin approval आवश्यक)");
}

/* ================= PRESS ================= */
function togglePress(){pressEditor.classList.toggle("hidden")}
function submitPress(){
  if(!pText.innerText.trim()){alert("Press खाली छ");return;}
  let img="";
  if(pFile.files[0]){
    let u=URL.createObjectURL(pFile.files[0]);
    img=`<img class="postimg ${pImgSize.value==='a4'?'a4':''}" src="${u}">`;
  }
  DB.press.push({
    html:sanitize(pText.innerHTML)+img,
    approved:false,
    hidden:false,
    date:Date.now()
  });
  saveDB();
  alert("Press saved");
}

/* ================= ADMIN ================= */
function login(){
  if(aid.value==="nhp_admin" && apass.value==="NHP@888"){
    sessionStorage.setItem("admin","1");
    loadAdmin();
  }else alert("Invalid login");
}
function logout(){
  sessionStorage.clear();
  adminPanel.classList.add("hidden");
}
function loadAdmin(){
  if(!sessionStorage.getItem("admin"))return;
  adminPanel.classList.remove("hidden");
  renderAdmin();
}

/* ================= ADMIN RENDER ================= */
function renderAdmin(){
  pending.innerHTML="";approved.innerHTML="";
  DB.voices.forEach((v,i)=>{
    let h=`<div class="post">
      <div class="status">Voice | ${new Date(v.date).toLocaleString()}</div>
      ${v.html}<br>
      ${!v.approved
        ?`<button onclick="approveV(${i})">Approve</button>`
        :`<button onclick="toggleHideV(${i})">${v.hidden?'Show':'Hide'}</button>`
      }
      <button class="red" onclick="delV(${i})">Delete</button>
    </div>`;
    (!v.approved?pending:approved).innerHTML+=h;
  });
  DB.press.forEach((p,i)=>{
    let h=`<div class="post">
      <div class="status">Press</div>
      ${p.html}<br>
      ${!p.approved
        ?`<button onclick="approveP(${i})">Approve</button>`
        :`<button onclick="toggleHideP(${i})">${p.hidden?'Show':'Hide'}</button>`
      }
      <button class="red" onclick="delP(${i})">Delete</button>
    </div>`;
    (!p.approved?pending:approved).innerHTML+=h;
  });
}

/* ================= ACTIONS ================= */
function approveV(i){DB.voices[i].approved=true;saveDB()}
function approveP(i){DB.press[i].approved=true;saveDB()}
function delV(i){DB.voices.splice(i,1);saveDB()}
function delP(i){DB.press.splice(i,1);saveDB()}
function toggleHideV(i){DB.voices[i].hidden=!DB.voices[i].hidden;saveDB()}
function toggleHideP(i){DB.press[i].hidden=!DB.press[i].hidden;saveDB()}

/* ================= SAVE + PUBLIC ================= */
function saveDB(){
  localStorage.setItem("voices",JSON.stringify(DB.voices));
  localStorage.setItem("press",JSON.stringify(DB.press));
  renderPublic();renderAdmin();
}
function renderPublic(){
  public.innerHTML="";
  DB.voices.filter(v=>v.approved&&!v.hidden).forEach(v=>{
    public.innerHTML+=`
    <div class="post">
      ${v.html}
      <div class="share">
        <a target="_blank" href="https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${location.href}">FB</a>
        <a target="_blank" href="https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(v.html)}">WA</a>
        <a target="_blank" href="https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=${encodeURIComponent(v.html)}">X</a>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  });
  DB.press.filter(p=>p.approved&&!p.hidden).forEach(p=>{
    public.innerHTML+=`<div class="post">${p.html}</div>`;
  });
}

renderPublic();loadAdmin();
</script>

  <div class="footer-bottom">
    © <span id="year"></span> नेपाल मानवतावादी पार्टी सर्वाधिकार सम्पन्न
  </div>
</footer>

<style>
:root{
  --red:#c4001d;
  --red-dark:#9b0016;
}

.site-footer{
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--red),var(--red-dark));
  padding:18px 14px 8px;       /* 🔥 ultra slim */
  color:#fff;
  border-top:2px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2);
}

.footer-inner{
  max-width:1150px;
  margin:auto;
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:1.3fr 1fr;
  gap:16px;
  align-items:center;
}

.footer-left{
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  gap:10px;
}

.logo,.flag{
  width:52px;                 /* 🔽 small */
  padding:3px;
  background:#fff;
  border-radius:8px;
}

.brand-text .ne{
  font-size:18px;
  font-weight:800;
  line-height:1.1;
}

.brand-text .en{
  font-size:11px;
  color:#ffd6db;
}

.footer-right{
  text-align:right;
  font-size:12.5px;
  line-height:1.5;
}

.contact-list div{
  margin:1px 0;
}

.contact-list i{
  color:#ffd6db;
  margin-right:5px;
}

.contact-list a{
  color:#fff;
  text-decoration:none;
}

.social-icons{
  margin-top:6px;
  display:flex;
  gap:8px;
  justify-content:flex-end;
}

.social-icons a{
  width:28px;
  height:28px;
  border-radius:50%;
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.15);
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  color:#fff;
  font-size:13px;
  transition:.2s;
}

.social-icons a:hover{
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--red);
}

.footer-bottom{
  margin-top:6px;
  text-align:center;
  font-size:11px;
  color:#ffe1e5;
}

@media(max-width:768px){
  .footer-inner{
    grid-template-columns:1fr;
    text-align:center;
  }
  .footer-right{text-align:center;}
  .social-icons{justify-content:center;}
}
</style>

<script>
document.getElementById("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
</script>












</body>
</html>
